&= 521
\end{aligned}
$

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-A5529866-6896-4634-A0C7-7BA27DF46995'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is halfway between $120$ and $300$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>


$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times (120 + 300) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 420\\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 210} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 210} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
&= 210
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$210$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times (120 + 300) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 420\\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 210} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 210} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
&= 210
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$210$
</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-FB6C0594-DADA-4D15-ACD2-FD4DCB46FFA0'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Krishn has $1 \ \text{hour}$ to clean the house before his parents get back from their holidays.

He has to hoover $3$ rooms, clean out his pet hamster's cage and wipe off the drawing of an elephant he accidently did on the bathroom wall.

Each room takes $12  \ \text{minutes}$ to hoover, it takes $18  \ \text{minutes}$ to clean out his hamster's cage and $21  \ \text{minutes}$ to rub out the elephant, which he does last.

Wht fraction of the elephant is left on the wall when Krishn's parents arrive home?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time left to wipe off the elephant}       &= 60 \ \text{min} - 12 \times 3 \ \text{min} - 18 \ \text{min} \\\\
                                                &= 60 - 36 - 18 \\\\
                                                &= 60 - 54 \\\\
                                                &= 6 \ \text{min}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Fraction of elephant left}                &= \dfrac {6} {21} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {3 \times 2} {3 \times 7} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {\cancel 3 \times 2} {\cancel 3 \times 7} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {2} {7}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {2} {7}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time left to wipe off the elephant}       &= 60 \ \text{min} - 12 \times 3 \ \text{min} - 18 \ \text{min} \\\\
                                                &= 60 - 36 - 18 \\\\
                                                &= 60 - 54 \\\\
                                                &= 6 \ \text{min}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Fraction of elephant left}                &= \dfrac {6} {21} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {3 \times 2} {3 \times 7} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {\cancel 3 \times 2} {\cancel 3 \times 7} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {2} {7}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {2} {7}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-FD79751E-764D-4640-AADD-196B4171B83D'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Abigail is three time as old as Ryan. In $6$ years time Abigail will be $42$.

How old will Ryan will be in $2$ years?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let Ryan's current age $= a$

Abigail's current age $= 3a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
3a + 6                      &= 42 \\\\
3a                          &= 42 - 6 \\\\
3a                          &= 36 \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {36} {3} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {12 \times 3} {3} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {12 \times \cancel {3}} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
a                           &= 12 \ \text{years} 
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Ryan's age after } 2 \text{ years }    &= 12 + 2 \\\\
                                            &= 14 \ \text{years}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$14 \ \text{years}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let Ryan's current age $= a$
<emptyline>
Abigail's current age $= 3a$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
3a + 6                      &= 42 \\\\
3a                          &= 42 - 6 \\\\
3a                          &= 36 \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {36} {3} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {12 \times 3} {3} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {12 \times \cancel {3}} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
a                           &= 12 \ \text{years} 
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Ryan's age after } 2 \text{ years }    &= 12 + 2 \\\\
                                            &= 14 \ \text{years}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$14 \ \text{years}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

What number is ten thousand more than $9876543$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 9876543 + 10000 \\\\
&= 9886543
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$9886543$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}

\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-49A3AC7D-1A5D-4648-9BA9-C13134C3645A'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Naheeda uses $10$ matchsticks to make a row of $3$ squares.
How many matchsticks would she need to make a row of $7$ squares?

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As shown in the figure,

To make a row of $n$ squares, we need $3n + 1$ matchsticks.

So,

For making a row of 7 squares

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 3 \times 7 + 1 \\\\
&= 21 + 1 \\\\
&= 22
\end{aligned}
$

She need $22$ matchsticks for making a row of 7 squares.

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$22$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As shown in the figure,
<emptyline>
To make a row of $n$ squares, we need $3n + 1$ matchsticks.
<emptyline>
So,
<emptyline>
For making a row of 7 squares
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= 3 \times 7 + 1 \\\\
&= 21 + 1 \\\\
&= 22
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
She need $22$ matchsticks for making a row of 7 squares.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$22$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>


</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is the most $4 \ \text{cm}$ by $4 \ \text{cm}$ flapjacks he could make?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$


As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of flapjacks}         &= \dfrac {\text{Area of slab}} {\text{Area of one flapjack}} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {20 \times 40} {4 \times 4} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {800} {16} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {16 \times 50} {16} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {\cancel {16} \times 50} {\cancel {16}} \\\\
                                   &= 50
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$50$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_katex_formula_area_rectangle_expanded
<emptyline>

<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}

                                   &= \dfrac {20 \times 40} {4 \times 4} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {800} {16} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {16 \times 50} {16} \\\\
                                   &= \dfrac {\cancel {16} \times 50} {\cancel {16}} \\\\
                                   &= 50
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$50$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-6C312D6E-654D-4791-935E-BC0C5352D615'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is $3 \dfrac{1}{2} \div \dfrac{1}{4}$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 3 \dfrac {1}{2} \div \dfrac{1}{4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7}{2} \times 4 \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times 4}{2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times 2 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
&= 7 \times 2 \\\\
&= 14
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$14$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 3 \dfrac {1}{2} \div \dfrac{1}{4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7}{2} \times 4 \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times 4}{2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times 2 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
&= 7 \times 2 \\\\
&= 14
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$14$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-587B8A90-626D-4702-8677-57FD78D2E7DD'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

*SPECIAL OFFER ON THE MATHS BOOKS!*

*Fun Trignometric Calculus For Pre-School, by Ian Tregation*

*Only $16$ pounds each.*

*Special offer: Buy $3$ and get $1$ free!*

Subhan wants to get $5$ of these books.

How much does he pay?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}  &= 3 \times 16 + 16 \\\\
                    &= 48 + 16 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 64
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 64$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}  &= 3 \times 16 + 16 \\\\
                    &= 48 + 16 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 64
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 64$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-04E60919-69C3-46EF-8B39-2318EE791A04'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Anokhi buys $4$ chocolate bars at $75 \text{p}$ each and $3$ pens at $95 \text{p}$ each.
How much change does she receive from a $10$ pounds note?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required
.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 4 \times 75 + 3 \times 95 \\\\
                        &= 300 + 285 \\\\
                        &= 585 \text{p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 5.85 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 10 - 5.85 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 4.15
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 4.15$

</div>

                        &= 585 \text{p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 5.85 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 10 - 5.85 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 4.15
\end{aligned}
$

<div class='question question'>

A "taxicab number" is a number that can be written as the sum  of two cube numbers in two different ways.

For example $1729$ is a taxicab number because
$1729 = 1 ^ {3} + 12 ^ {3}$ and also $1729 = 9 ^ {3} + 10 ^ {3}$

Zain says "There are no taxicab number less than $30$." Is he right?

Explain your reasoning carefully.

</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-7002E449-EEBA-49EF-A1BD-DA0806D4924B'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is $30.03 - 3.3$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: Remember to line up the decimal points.


$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
    &   3   &   0 \ .    &    0  &   3 \\
{}- &       &   3 \ .    &    3  &   0 \\
\hline
    &   2   &   6 \ .    &    7  &   3
\end{array}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$26.73$

</div>
</div>

<div class='workingcodeblock'>

\end{array}
$

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-2233EE67-0456-49F5-B1A4-331DDBD7F697'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Theo thinks of a number and finds out  that three quarters of it is $24$.

What was the number he first thought of?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the number Theo first thought of $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {3} {4} \ \text{of} \ a &= 24 \\\\
\dfrac {3} {4} \times a        &= 24 \\\\
\dfrac {3a} {4}                &= 24 \\\\
a                              &= \dfrac{24 \times 4} {3} \\\\
a                              &= \dfrac{8 \times 3 \times 4} {3} \\\\
a                              &= \dfrac{8 \times \cancel {3} \times 4} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
a                              &= 8 \times 4 \\\\
a                              &= 32
\end{aligned}
$

The number Theo first thought of $= 32$.

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$32$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the number Theo first thought of $= a$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {3} {4} \ \text{of} \ a &= 24 \\\\
\dfrac {3} {4} \times a        &= 24 \\\\
\dfrac {3a} {4}                &= 24 \\\\
a                              &= \dfrac{24 \times 4} {3} \\\\
a                              &= \dfrac{8 \times 3 \times 4} {3} \\\\
a                              &= \dfrac{8 \times \cancel {3} \times 4} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
a                              &= 8 \times 4 \\\\
a                              &= 32
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
The number Theo first thought of $= 32$.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$32$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is $0.32$ as a fraction? Give your answer in its simplest form.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 0.32 \\\\
&= \dfrac {32} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8 \times 4} {25 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8 \times \cancel {4}} {25 \times \cancel {4}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8} {25}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {8} {25}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 0.32 \\\\
&= \dfrac {32} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8 \times 4} {25 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8 \times \cancel {4}} {25 \times \cancel {4}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8} {25}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {8} {25}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is eighteen fortieths as a decimal?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {18} {40} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9 \times 2} {20 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9 \times 2 \times 5} {20 \times 2 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9 \times \cancel 2 \times 5} {20 \times \cancel 2 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {45} {100} \\\\
&= 0.45
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$0.45$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {18} {40} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9 \times 2} {20 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9 \times 2 \times 5} {20 \times 2 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9 \times \cancel 2 \times 5} {20 \times \cancel 2 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {45} {100} \\\\
&= 0.45
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-4751D63D-6131-4E1E-BD76-5F3BF5800D72'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is $30\%$ of $1600$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 30 \% \ \text {of} \ 1600 \\\\
&= \dfrac {30} {100} \times 1600 \\\\
&= \dfrac {30 \times 1600} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {30 \times 16 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
&= 30 \times 16 \\\\
&= 480
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$480$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 30 \% \ \text {of} \ 1600 \\\\
&= \dfrac {30} {100} \times 1600 \\\\
&= \dfrac {30 \times 1600} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {30 \times 16 \cancel {00}} {1 \cancel {00}} \\\\
&= 30 \times 16 \\\\
&= 480
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$480$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-023389AE-3402-4C39-A59D-560327B4A49B'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

One quire of paper is equal to $24$ sheets.
One ream is equal to $20$ quires.
How many sheets are there in $2$ ream?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of Sheets in two ream}    &= 2 \times 20 \times 24 \\\\
                                        &= 40 \times 24 \\\\
                                        &= 960
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$960$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of Sheets in two ream}    &= 2 \times 20 \times 24 \\\\
                                        &= 40 \times 24 \\\\
                                        &= 960
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$960$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-329E7FE5-9B8D-4D16-8082-F798504B6844'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is $0.2 \times 0.3$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: Let's first multiply the numbers ignoring the decimal points.


$2 \times 3 = 6$

Between the two numbers, the total number of digits after the decimal point is two.

Let's account for the decimal point now. 

$= 0.06$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$0.06$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_multiply_when_decimal_points_note
<emptyline>
$2 \times 3 = 6$
<emptyline>
Between the two numbers, the total number of digits after the decimal point is two.
<emptyline>
Let's account for the decimal point now. 
<emptyline>
$= 0.06$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$0.06$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How many centimetres are there in $1.4 \ \text{metres}$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1.4 \ \text {metres} \\\\
&= 1.4 \times 100 \ \text {centimetres} \\\\
&= 140 \ \text {centimetres}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$140 \ \text {centimetres}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1.4 \ \text {metres} \\\\
&= 1.4 \times 100 \ \text {centimetres} \\\\
&= 140 \ \text {centimetres}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$140 \ \text {centimetres}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How many millimetres are there in $2.3  \ \text{kilometres}$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2.3 \ \text {kilometres} \\\\
&= 2.3 \times 1000 \ \text {meters} \\\\
&= 2.3 \times 1000 \times 1000 \ \text {millimetres} \\\\
&= 2300000 \ \text {millimetres}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2300000 \ \text {millimetres}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2.3 \ \text {kilometres} \\\\
&= 2.3 \times 1000 \ \text {meters} \\\\
&= 2.3 \times 1000 \times 1000 \ \text {millimetres} \\\\
&= 2300000 \ \text {millimetres}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$2300000 \ \text {millimetres}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

Three prime numbers multiply together to get $110$. What are they?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Three prime numbers $= 2, 5, 11$

Let's verify.

$
\begin {aligned}
&= 2 \times 5 \times 11 \\\\
&= 10 \times 11 \\\\
&= 110
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2, 5, 11$

</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-1C0336ED-FF75-46F7-A592-23696B697EF3'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Amrit, Brenda and Clare share $13$ sweets. Amrit gets three times as many as Brenda, and Brenda get three times as many as Clare. How many do they each get?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the number of sweets Clare get $= a$.

Number of sweets Brenda get $= 3a$.

Number of sweets Amrit get $= 9a$.

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 3a + 9a     &= 13 \\\\
4a + 9a         &= 13 \\\\
13a             &= 13 \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {13} {13} \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {\cancel {13}} {\cancel {13}} \\\\
a               &= 1
\end{aligned}
$

Number of sweets Clare get $= 1$.

Number of sweets Brenda get $= 3 \times 1 = 3$.

Number of sweets Amrit get $= 9 \times 1 = 9$..


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

Amrit $=$ $9$

</div>
<div class='answer'>

Brenda $=$ $3$

</div>
<div class='answer'>

Clare $=$ $1$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the number of sweets Clare get $= a$.
<emptyline>
Number of sweets Brenda get $= 3a$.
<emptyline>
Number of sweets Amrit get $= 9a$.
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
a + 3a + 9a     &= 13 \\\\
4a + 9a         &= 13 \\\\
13a             &= 13 \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {13} {13} \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {\cancel {13}} {\cancel {13}} \\\\
a               &= 1
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Number of sweets Clare get $= 1$.
<emptyline>
Number of sweets Brenda get $= 3 \times 1 = 3$.
<emptyline>
Number of sweets Amrit get $= 9 \times 1 = 9$..
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Amrit</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$9$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Brenda</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$3$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Clare</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-395B7BEF-3DD7-44D2-946A-7CA43359808F'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Gina finished her homework at $8{:}24 \ \text{pm}$ having started at $6{:}52 \ \text{pm}$.
How many minutes did she spend on it?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time spent}          &= 8{:}24 \ \text{pm} - 6{:}52 \ \text{pm} \\\\
                              &= 1 \ \text{hour} \ 32 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                              &= 1 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 32  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                              &= 60 + 32 \\\\
                              &= 92 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$92 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time spent}          &= 8{:}24 \ \text{pm} - 6{:}52 \ \text{pm} \\\\
                              &= 1 \ \text{hour} \ 32 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                              &= 1 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 32  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                              &= 60 + 32 \\\\
                              &= 92 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$92 \ \text{minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-9084E70D-57B3-42F0-867B-558AB1041DE5'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A rectangle has an area of $20  \ \text{cm}^{2}$. Its sides are doubled to make a new rectangle.
What is the area of new rectangle?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$


Let the length of the rectangle $= l$

Let the width of the rectangle $= w$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Area of rectanlge}   &= 20 \\\\
l \times w                  &= 20 \ \text {cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$

After sides are doubled,

New length $= 2 \times l$

New width $= 2 \times w$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {New area}    &= (2 \times l) \times (2 \times w) \\\\
                    &= 4 \times (l \times w) \\\\
                    &= 4 \times 20 \ \text {cm} ^2 \\\\
                    &= 80 \ \text {cm} ^2
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$80 \ \text {cm} ^2$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_katex_formula_area_rectangle_expanded
<emptyline>
Let the length of the rectangle $= l$
<emptyline>
Let the width of the rectangle $= w$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Area of rectanlge}   &= 20 \\\\
l \times w                  &= 20 \ \text {cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
After sides are doubled,
<emptyline>
New length $= 2 \times l$
<emptyline>
New width $= 2 \times w$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {New area}    &= (2 \times l) \times (2 \times w) \\\\
                    &= 4 \times (l \times w) \\\\
                    &= 4 \times 20 \ \text {cm} ^2 \\\\
                    &= 80 \ \text {cm} ^2
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$80 \ \text {cm} ^2$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

                                &= \dfrac {54 - 28} { 54} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {26} { 54} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {2 \times 13} {2 \times 27} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times 13} {\cancel{2} \times 27} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {13} {27}
\end{aligned}
$

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-963BEC59-6DAD-42B4-A251-A32822C591F6'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The first term of the sequence is $50$, the second is $46$ and the third is $42$, and so on.
How many terms are there until the first negative term?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

The sequence is :

$50, \quad 46, \quad 42, \quad 38, \quad 34, \quad 30, \quad 26, \quad 22, \quad 18, \quad 14, \quad 10, \quad 6, \quad 2, \quad -2$

By counting these terms we get,

$10$

terms are required to reach the first negative term.

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$10$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>The sequence is :
<emptyline>
$50, \quad 46, \quad 42, \quad 38, \quad 34, \quad 30, \quad 26, \quad 22, \quad 18, \quad 14, \quad 10, \quad 6, \quad 2, \quad -2$
<emptyline>
By counting these terms we get,
<emptyline>
$10$
<emptyline>
terms are required to reach the first negative term.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$10$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-E5D37E54-20E2-4DB6-83F2-B10511CE4323'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Jaishan notices that if he squares two numbers and find the difference  of his answer he gets the same result as multiplying the sum and the difference of the two number.

For example, if he chooses $9$ and $3$:   

$9 ^ {2} - 3 ^ {2} = 81 - 9 = 72$ and 

$(9 + 3) \times (9-3) = 12 \times 6 = 72$

Show that this works with the number $6$ and $2$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Square of numbers}                &= 6 ^ {2} - 2 ^ {2} \\\\
                                        &= 36 - 4 \\\\
                                        &= 32 \\\\
\text{Product of sum \& difference}      &= (6 + 2) \times (6 - 2) \\\\
                                        &= 8 \times 4 \\\\
                                        &= 32
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$32$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Square of numbers}                &= 6 ^ {2} - 2 ^ {2} \\\\
                                        &= 36 - 4 \\\\
                                        &= 32 \\\\
\text{Product of sum \& difference}      &= (6 + 2) \times (6 - 2) \\\\
                                        &= 8 \times 4 \\\\
                                        &= 32
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$32$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Jaishan uses this to find $83 \times 83 - 17 \times 17$ **WITHOUT** using long multiplication.
How did he do it and what is the answer?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Using the above method,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 83 ^ {2} - 17 ^ {2} \\\\
&= (83 + 17) \times (83 - 17) \\\\
&= 100 \times 56 \\\\
&= 5600
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$5600$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Using the above method,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= 83 ^ {2} - 17 ^ {2} \\\\
&= (83 + 17) \times (83 - 17) \\\\
&= 100 \times 56 \\\\
&= 5600
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$5600$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-A8E9E000-1E86-461F-B304-C36B53D42D9F'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Bob has $15$ more Match Dfex cards than Alan.

Colin gives Bob $25$ cards. Bob now has $3$ times as many as Alan.

How many has Alan got?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the number of cards Alan has $= a$

Number of cards Bob has $= a + 15$

As per the question,

$
\begin {aligned}
a + 15 + 25 &= 3a \\\\
a + 40      &= 3a \\\\
3a - a      &= 40 \\\\
2a          &= 40 \\\\
a           &= \dfrac {40} {2} \\\\
a           &= \dfrac {20 \times 2} {2} \\\\
a           &= \dfrac {20 \times \cancel 2} {\cancel 2} \\\\
a           &= 20
\end {aligned}
$

Number of cards Alan has $= 20$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$20$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the number of cards Alan has $= a$
<emptyline>
Number of cards Bob has $= a + 15$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin {aligned}
a + 15 + 25 &= 3a \\\\
a + 40      &= 3a \\\\
3a - a      &= 40 \\\\
2a          &= 40 \\\\
a           &= \dfrac {40} {2} \\\\
a           &= \dfrac {20 \times 2} {2} \\\\
a           &= \dfrac {20 \times \cancel 2} {\cancel 2} \\\\
a           &= 20
\end {aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Number of cards Alan has $= 20$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$20$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
